Full root Go tests/vet, standalone RelayKit tests/vet, and focused race tests passed. The commits are not pushed, published, or deployed.

- On 2026-08-24, forced OpenAI response formatting was fixed to retain upstream `choices[].logprobs`, including `content` and `reasoning_content` arrays, via commit `7917eb52b`. This preserves real provider logprobs without synthesizing values; focused helper tests and full root/RelayKit tests passed. The commit is local and not deployed.
- On 2026-08-24, review found Midjourney actions based on an existing task could overwrite the distributor's selected channel with the stored origin channel after policy checks. `RelayMidjourneySubmit` now loads the subject-group policy and validates the stored channel/model before forwarding an action; ordinary task/result fetches remain unchanged. Focused relay/service/middleware tests passed; the fix is local and not deployed.
- On 2026-08-24, relay failover now distinguishes credential and channel failures. Multi-key channels keep `401/403/429` retries on the same channel and rotate only untried keys without consuming the global retry slot; non-key upstream failures, including upstream `400` and `5xx`, exclude the current channel before the next retry. Local request errors remain non-retryable. Focused controller/service/model tests passed; the change is local and not deployed.
- On 2026-08-24, review of the relay failover change fixed the last-key boundary: exhausting a multi-key channel cancels the pending retry-counter reset before moving to the next channel, and affinity selection skips channels already excluded by the current request. Full root Go tests/vet and standalone RelayKit tests/vet passed; the follow-up is local and not deployed.
- On 2026-08-24, follow-up review fixed task failover gaps: locked origin-task submissions now release the channel lock for non-key failures and after multi-key exhaustion, while retaining the lock for same-channel `401/403/429` key rotation. Affinity `skip_retry_on_failure` no longer blocks those multi-key credential retries. Focused controller/service tests and vet passed; the follow-up is local and not deployed.
- On 2026-08-24, relay failover fixes from `79ecf12ea86192cc68fd226ff6b358e594ae96ea` were published as `v1.0.0-rc.25-tokeness-relay-failover.1` at immutable GHCR digest `sha256:782e1836ee84c65f603e0e619882e7b88393f686eb71c067ce7e4ba682c09099`. Publish workflow `32723127423`, baseline verify `32724255841`, staged production deploy `32724425190`, and post-deploy verify `32724942447` all passed. JP-N2, EV-JP, JP-M, and EV-JP2 selected and ran the new digest; all reported `running` with healthy or expected no-healthcheck status. `tokeness.io/` and `/api/status` returned 200, while `n.tokeness.io` and `n.tokeness.cn` `/v1/models` returned the expected unauthenticated 401 with the new version header. The previous Ollama-hardening digest `sha256:c42879e42971b94330fce459d895f3e57ca9e892fccfd57f82c001bca1c8c9ac` remains the rollback reference.
- On 2026-08-24, production compatibility acceptance for the authorized DeepSeek V4 test matrix passed on both `n.tokeness.io/v1` and `n-cf.tokeness.io/v1` running `v1.0.0-rc.25-tokeness-relay-failover.1`. Basic, streaming with `include_usage`, tools, stop, and logprobs requests returned 200; stream responses included a final usage event; tools returned a function tool call; stop returned the expected prefix with `finish_reason=stop`; logprobs exposed both `content` and `reasoning_content` arrays with no position exceeding five `top_logprobs` entries. `reasoning_effort=extreme` and `top_p=1.5` were accepted and normalized. The DFLASH capability error did not surface in the live sample because requests succeeded through an available route; its 400 capability classification and retry-without-channel-disable behavior passed focused local tests. One primary-node stop response transiently ended with `length` during the first sample, but three immediate repeats on both nodes returned the expected stop result. The test credential was not stored or logged.

It separates official DeepSeek behavior, OpenAI-compatible response shape, and Tokeness retry/failover/billing contracts; covers deterministic T0/T1 tests plus official and main/backup route tiers; and records `extreme`, invalid `top_p`, reasoning/tool multi-turn validation, logprobs/DFLASH capability errors, key rotation, channel failover, locked tasks, usage settlement, and redaction. The specification contains no credential values and has not yet been executed as a new runner.

This protects compatible upstreams that ignore the standalone thinking toggle; the raw `thinking` field remains preserved. DeepSeek adapter and relay test suites passed; the change is local and not deployed.
- On 2026-08-25, the DeepSeek V4 provider/gateway compliance follow-up classified the current result as partial rather than full compliance. - On 2026-08-25, the DeepSeek V4 compatibility candidate added typed channel-selection outcomes before the distributor error boundary. Unknown/unconfigured models are classified as `model_not_configured` and map to `400 model_not_found`; configured but unavailable channels map to `503 get_channel_failed`; policy-blocked requests map to `403 access_denied`; missing-channel/database consistency errors map to `500 get_channel_failed`. Auto-group diagnosis uses the same compact-alias/path resolution as selection, and regression tests cover normal auto selection, disabled channels, policy-denied channels, and dangling Ability references. The probe remains partial at 25/84 implemented live fixture IDs, with 59 cases inconclusive.
- On 2026-08-25, production diagnosis found that the first relay retry can receive a temporary `model.Channel` from `getChannel` before `RelayInfo.ChannelMeta` is initialized. Because that object lacks `ChannelInfo.IsMultiKey`, an initial multi-key channel `429` is misclassified as a channel failure: the channel is excluded and the global retry counter is consumed instead of rotating the next untried key on the same channel. With `CN_OpenCode-GO_A` (priority 9) excluded, retry index 1 selects the second remaining priority (`CN_DeepSeek`, priority 7), skipping enabled `CN_OpenCode-GO_B` (priority 8). Correlated production logs showed `channel_id=93`, `multi_key_index=7`, status 429, followed by `use_channel=["93","1"]`; B was enabled but not attempted, and no other A key was tried. This was diagnosis only; no production code or deployment changed. A regression test should cover the uninitialized-`ChannelMeta` first-attempt path and assert same-channel key rotation without consuming the global retry slot.
- On 2026-08-25, the DeepSeek V4 probe was extended to 29/85 live fixture IDs. Chat request validation now rejects V4 `top_logprobs` without `logprobs=true` and values outside `[0,20]` with the official 400 `invalid_request_error` envelope (`param: null`); other models are unchanged. Probe evidence stores only a short normalized error-message fingerprint, and thinking logprobs assert both `content` and `reasoning_content` arrays when the provider supports them. Existing forced OpenAI response formatting preserves both arrays without synthesizing missing reasoning logprobs. Focused helper/RelayKit/probe tests passed; no credentials were stored and nothing was deployed.
- On 2026-08-25, the first-attempt multi-key failover regression was fixed locally. The distributor preserves the selected full `model.Channel` in request context, so `getChannel` retains `IsMultiKey` before relay metadata exists; `401/403/429` now rotate untried keys on `CN_OpenCode-GO_A` without consuming the global retry slot. After A is exhausted, the request excludes it and restarts selection at the highest remaining priority, selecting `CN_OpenCode-GO_B` before lower-priority channels. Selection coverage includes memory-cache, database, auto-group, and later-group-error paths; `go test ./...`, `go vet ./...`, and `git diff --check` passed. This is local only and has not been committed, published, or deployed.
- On 2026-08-25, the DeepSeek V4 fit follow-up normalized middleware errors to an OpenAI-compatible public envelope without exposing `new_api_error`: authentication uses `authentication_error` / `invalid_request_error`, validation carries `param: null`, and 5xx middleware failures use `server_error`; typed channel-selection details remain internal. The fit probe now requires null error params, accepts valid disabled-thinking tool calls without requiring text content, parses both legal SSE `data:` forms, treats gateway quota failures as failures rather than official-provider inconclusive results, and requires explicit opt-in before calling the official route. A redacted main-route repeat confirmed production still accepts invalid `top_logprobs` until deployment; thinking-disabled, complex SSE, tool, and repeated stop cases passed. The named-message 256-token case was isolated as reasoning budget exhaustion rather than a `name` incompatibility: named and unnamed variants both ended at 256 tokens with `finish_reason=length` and often no final content, while 1024 tokens or disabled thinking returned content. The probe records protocol acceptance separately from effective success. Focused tests passed; no response bodies or credential values were stored, and nothing was deployed.
- On 2026-08-25, the typed channel-selection follow-up fixed three remaining local classification edges: Advanced Custom no-route results now reuse request-path filtering instead of being mislabeled as temporary capacity loss; compact alias resolution excludes policy-blocked channels so an exact blocked alias can fall back to an allowed base model; and an uninitialized memory channel cache returns an internal selection error instead of being diagnosed from the database as a 503. Regression tests cover path mismatch, blocked compact alias fallback, and cache readiness. Focused model/service/middleware/controller tests and `git diff --check` passed. The changes are local only and have not been published or deployed.
- On 2026-08-25, the DeepSeek V4 fit candidate completed the strict validation and empty-output follow-up locally. V4 `reasoning_effort=extreme`, invalid `top_p`, `tool_choice=required`, and invalid `top_logprobs` now return official-shaped 400 validation errors instead of being normalized or clamped. Chat Completions responses with neither non-empty final content nor a valid function tool call are rejected as `502 server_error`; committed streams mark the error `skip_retry`, while valid tool-only output remains successful and legacy Completions mode is not affected. Thinking logprobs require both `content` and `reasoning_content` arrays when enabled; missing reasoning logprobs remain a typed unsupported-feature result and are never synthesized. The feature-probe specification was updated to remove the old clamp/capability-superset wording. - On 2026-08-25, multi-key relay retries were tightened so upstream `401` errors exclude the current channel and advance to the next channel instead of rotating every key on the same channel. `403` and `429` retain same-channel key rotation; the previous unbounded-looking retry chain was caused by resetting the global retry counter for those internal key attempts. Commit `2a8016700` passed the release regression suite and was published as `v1.0.0-rc.25-tokeness-multikey-401.1` at immutable GHCR digest `sha256:5984f6f6757dc4aa2aa1aeb95485ad41031aee34f073ce8666ecb58ce8f5a44c`; publish workflow `32846527584` and staged production deploy `32847558130` passed. All four nodes and `tokeness.ai`/`n.tokeness.dev`/`n-cf.tokeness.dev` routes exposed the new version; dashboard probes returned 200 and unauthenticated API probes returned the expected 401. Focused controller retry tests and the release regression suite passed.

- On 2026-08-25, the production performance audit confirmed the existing isolated `scripts/performance` harness cannot measure real DeepSeek V4 KV-cache hit rate, effective success, TTFT P90, TPOT P90, or TPM: it uses a deterministic mock upstream, counts every 2xx as success, drops response bodies and SSE timestamps, fixes the model to `gpt-4o-mini`, and uses a 30-second timeout. A small production run therefore requires a separate transient-key load generator that records only structural usage/timing evidence; no usable runtime credential was present in the process, so no production load was started and no performance target is claimed.
- On 2026-08-26, the K01-K13 acceptance runner and V4 response boundary were audited and repaired locally. The Go `fit` profile sends the exact supplied request bodies, requires exact HTTP 400 and official logprobs error fingerprints, validates official non-stream/SSE/usage/logprobs schemas, and gates every gateway `pass` on a passing same-status official baseline. K04/K06/K07 now accept the official reasoning-only `finish_reason=length` truncation, and the relay preserves that response when thinking is enabled; disabled thinking strips both reasoning text and reasoning logprobs. V4 streams no longer emit a successful `[DONE]` without a finish reason, and fitted usage is normalized to non-negative, arithmetic-consistent official fields. Full root tests/vet, standalone RelayKit test/build, focused race tests, and `git diff --check` passed. With gateway and official API-key environment variables explicitly empty, the fit runner produced 39/39 `inconclusive` records and made no live request. No authenticated K01-K13 production verdict, commit, publication, or deployment is claimed; no credential or response body was stored.
- On 2026-08-26, stream failures reported as `stream state: error eof` were traced to two compatibility cases: the scanner mishandled bare `[DONE]`, and some OpenAI-compatible upstreams closed after a non-empty terminal `finish_reason` without sending `[DONE]`. The scanner now accepts bare and `data:` DONE markers, drains all chunk workers before final classification, promotes only terminal-finish EOF to normal completion, and keeps genuinely truncated streams as 502 with skip-retry. DeepSeek V4 regression coverage also rejects DONE without a finish reason and preserves the terminal usage/finish chunk shape. No production credential or response body was stored.
- On 2026-08-26, the production `stream state: error eof` incident was traced to Tokeness commit `f4f65939d`, which changed New API's upstream-compatible clean EOF from normal to `server_error` and required `[DONE]`/`finish_reason` in `OaiStreamHandler`. Official new-api treats clean EOF, `[DONE]`, and handler stop as normal completion. Commit `fdbbbd6503` restored that behavior while retaining scanner errors, timeouts, client disconnects, upstream error events, and empty-output rejection. It was published as `v1.0.0-rc.25-tokeness-stream-eof.2` at immutable digest `sha256:636f67c3aa930c2dd6b274d07333476b43edf91dafa9dacf2184afdcbf66cbd9` by workflow `32949087397`. The first rollout `32949803929` failed because EV-JP's 15 GB root disk was full; the failed atomic write left `release.env` empty while the old container remained healthy. Unused Docker images and archived journals were removed, `release.env` was atomically restored from the running immutable image, and all four nodes were verified on the old baseline before retrying. Rollout `32954050267` then deployed the target digest to all four nodes and passed public route checks. A real temporary-token SSE acceptance returned HTTP 200 with `[DONE]`, usage, and `finish_reason=stop`; its usage log recorded `stream_status=ok`, `end_reason=done`, and the token was deleted. In the first 8.5 minutes after deployment, 850 stream logs contained 610 normal EOFs and zero `error/eof`; residual errors were 60 HTTP/2 scanner failures for `claude-opus-5` channel 76, three canceled scans for `gpt-5.6-sol` channel 33, and nine client disconnects, which are separate from this incident.
- On 2026-08-26, a read-only production investigation of `claude-opus-5` stream rows showed a separate incident from the fixed clean-EOF regression. The two reported rows ran on EV-JP2 and ended on Anthropic channel 76 (`Claude_apijing`, `apijing.com`) with Go HTTP/2 reader errors `INTERNAL_ERROR; received from peer`; each received three SSE data events, then failed about 125 seconds after the first event. One request first hit channel 78, which returned HTTP 400 because `temperature` is deprecated for this model, before fallback to channel 76. From 17:23 through the final audit snapshot, every channel-76 `scanner_error` belonged to one user/token (`token_id=14018`, token name `kc`) and one source IP, indicating a client/workload-specific retry storm rather than a platform-wide outage; repeated failed attempts wrote large Claude cache prefixes and accumulated billable usage. The deployed OpenAI-compatible handler records `scanner_error` in consume-log metadata but, after any usable output, still finalizes with synthetic `[DONE]`, HTTP 200, and normal settlement because it no longer gates completion on `StreamStatus`; clean EOF compatibility and real scanner/timeout failures therefore need separate handling in any fix. Channel 76 currently uses automatic HTTP negotiation with one connection shard. No production configuration or data was changed.
- On 2026-08-26, the upstream CDN billing incident was fixed in commit `fc57be947` and deployed as `v1.0.0-rc.25-tokeness-stream-billing.1` at immutable GHCR digest `sha256:8b6c31e15ab5496c8dac8364fb658834d0df3959b0c9a7fb543a4728b9c17630` by publish workflow `32967987062` and staged rollout `32968999167`. Incomplete OpenAI-compatible streams now return 502 `server_error`, preserve `scanner_error`/termination reason, never emit synthetic `[DONE]`, and skip retry after any upstream SSE data to avoid duplicating provider charges. Observed usage is settled before the outer refund path; `BillingSession.Refund` is idempotently a no-op after settlement. Missing terminal usage uses the existing partial-input/output estimate; partial tool calls retain surcharges, audio-token streams use audio settlement, and zero-token partial streams remain auditable. Reader failures before any upstream data remain retryable/refundable. Release tests, `go test ./...`, `go vet ./...`, and `git diff --check` passed. All four nodes and `tokeness.ai`, `n.tokeness.dev`, `n-cf.tokeness.dev` passed rollout checks; the public API routes returned expected 401 after a transient CDN 520 during immediate post-deploy probing.
- On 2026-08-26, production Claude routing was mitigated without touching secrets: channel 76 (`Claude_apijing`) remains disabled after the HTTP/2 CDN reset storm; channel 78 (`Claude_gwlink_Ex`) was updated through the management path to priority `479` with `param_override` deleting both `temperature` and `top_p`, while channel 62 remains enabled at priority `478` as fallback. After 75 seconds of cache convergence, channel 78 handled 288 requests with 269 successes (93.4%) in the observed hour; channel 62 handled 1,051 requests with 689 successes (65.6%) and still has an independent upstream failure rate requiring follow-up.
- On 2026-08-31, wallet pre-consume settlement fix commit `1ff87124b3fb73a041ca1baf85149858e1e86963` was published as `v1.0.0-rc.25-tokeness-wallet-preconsume.1` at immutable GHCR digest `sha256:b9a3ea449949b5f69c450ee682677c4d8c1a4dc4ecdb452deb6e3571df3b7a0a` by publish workflow `33353939793`. Production deployment workflow `33354454406` approved the `tokeness-production` environment and completed the staged rollout successfully. All four nodes and CDN routes reported the target version; `https://tokeness.ai/` and `/api/status` returned 200, while `https://n.tokeness.dev` and `https://n-cf.tokeness.dev` returned the expected unauthenticated 401. The rollout reported one active old-slot connection that did not drain within 120 seconds and deferred old blue-green container cleanup; the new slot was committed and healthy. This memory entry was recorded after deployment in a documentation-only follow-up commit.
- On 2026-08-31, read-only PostgreSQL analysis of test token `T` identified the customer `deepseek-v4-pro` load at `09:57-09:58 Asia/Shanghai`: 300 unique request IDs, 535 upstream attempts, prompt-token/cache-token ratio `18,341,130 / 11,597,741 = 63.23%`, and request-level FRT P50/P90 of `2.29s / 29.19s`. There were 235 upstream HTTP 429 attempts across 170 requests. Initial routing sent 188 requests to channel 93 (`CN_OpenCode-GO_A`), all with a successful terminal marker; 43 to channel 133 (`CN_Ollama_A`) and 69 to channel 134 (`CN_Pipio`), where only 42 eventually reached a successful terminal marker and 70 had no success/error terminal marker. Channel 133 partial/no-terminal attempts had FRT around 31-38s at P50/P90, matching the reported TTFT tail; channel 134 produced 72 HTTP 429 responses. Channel 93 credential indices 13 and 38 produced 86 and 53 HTTP 429 attempts respectively, while index 8 had 133 successful attempts. The retry sequences (`134->93`, `133->133`, and repeated `93`) show retry amplification and unstable route/cache locality. At the time of the audit, the database model list for channel 134 no longer included `deepseek-v4-pro`, although it appeared in the incident request path; verify channel-cache convergence or a post-incident configuration change before treating this as a current selection bug. No production configuration or code was changed.

- On 2026-08-31, a read-only post-routing comparison using test token `T` isolated a second, downstream latency problem. With a fixed approximately 60k-token prefix, streaming requests through `n.tokeness.dev` reached channel 93 and New API recorded HTTP 200, `stream_status=ok`, `end_reason=done`, `finish_reason=stop`, `frt=1.086-1.396s`, and `cache_tokens=60032` of `prompt_tokens=60088`; the 16-request controlled run had 17/17 successful terminal records and zero HTTP 429. The client nevertheless measured approximately 273s to the first SSE chunk and 273.9s total. The same request through `n-cf.tokeness.dev` returned HTTP 403 after approximately 271s, produced no SSE or usage, and created no New API consumption log, proving that failure is before the application. The JP-M `aib-s2a.swhub.cn` access/error logs were stale (last write Aug 7/Aug 21), so they are not the active `.dev` API ingress evidence. This places the current bottleneck in EdgeOne/Cloudflare edge SSE buffering, origin connection handling, or WAF/timeout policy, not DeepSeek inference, channel 93, or New API FRT. No production configuration or code was changed. Recommended remediation: configure both CDN routes for unbuffered HTTP/1.1 SSE pass-through with no cache/compression transformation, long origin/read/idle timeouts, and WAF rules that allow authenticated `POST /v1/chat/completions`; then validate with a small single-request canary measuring first-byte, first-SSE, and terminal times separately. Keep channel 93 as the preferred route, but do not use additional load until the edge path is fixed.
- On 2026-09-02, the "Ollama cache never hits" production report was diagnosed from the shared primary node (JP-M hosts the shared PostgreSQL as Docker container `1Panel-postgresql-8PwR`, database `new-api`; the `logs.other` JSON carries `admin_info.ollama_prompt_cache` with outcome/family/partition_hash/message_chain_hash, and Redis container `1Panel-redis-b5CC` stores keys under `ollama_prompt_cache:v1:<partition>`). Findings: the estimator works for normal text sessions (root user session on channel 133 reached 46/47 estimated hits); "user id 0" corresponds to channel-test relay attempts which show `Token{Id: 0}` in container logs and never accumulate hits because channel tests rotate the multi-key credentials (CredentialId 226/231/232 for indexes 0/1/2) so each test request lands in a different prompt-cache partition and test prompts are one-shot. Two long ZCode agent sessions (partitions 242df9eda and bfa5f037, 128K-320K prompt tokens) were permanently `uncacheable`: their early requests carried up to 8 screenshot image parts, and although the per-request model-io message tail later showed zero images, the upstream prompt token counts never dropped when the images left the logged 64-message tail, proving ZCode sends a longer message window than its model-io log displays, so the old screenshots remain in the final Ollama body and every request keeps tripping the images-uncacheable rule. Estimator behavior is per design (images/keep_alive=0/unreadable bodies are uncacheable and store nothing). One customer token (user 14015, "Hy3") showed exact-replay requests (identical message_chain_hash 16s apart) landing on two different partition hashes at the same multi_key_index, indicating that client sends per-request-varying session identity or request parameters; by-design isolation makes those never hit. A local replay harness `relay/channel/ollama/repro_prompt_cache_test.go` (skipped unless REPRO_REQ is set) now exists to run captured client request fixtures through the real conversion + prompt-cache identity pipeline; it proved `reasoning` content parts and the raw internal message format would fail `openAIChatToOllamaChat`, i.e. the real wire serializes reasoning as `reasoning_content`.
- On 2026-09-02, Ollama prompt cache estimation was extended so multimodal requests can be estimated: chat messages hash images as per-message SHA-256 content digests (`hashPromptCacheMessage` / `promptCacheImageDigests`) and generate requests fold an images digest into the prompt+suffix identity, replacing the previous whole-request uncacheable rule for image-bearing bodies. Raw image data is still never serialized into keys, candidates, keyMaterial, or logs; changed or removed images no longer match. This fixes the production finding where long ZCode sessions with screenshots (old screenshots still present in the actually-sent window even after the model-io message tail showed none) were permanently uncacheable. The `ollama_prompt_cache` observation now carries a `cause` (`no_user`, `keep_alive_zero`, `unreadable_body`, `empty_messages`) on uncacheable outcomes. Channel-test requests still never accumulate hits because multi-key tests rotate credentials (by-design partition isolation). The change is local and uncommitted; focused ollama tests, race tests, vet, full build, and `git diff --check` pass.
- **2026-09-05 扣费路径确认：DB 是权威，Redis 只是缓存（多实例独立 sidecar Redis 安全）**：回答「redis 有必要共享吗」而做的代码追踪。结论：**不共享，每实例独立 Redis 完全正确**。证据：(1) `model/quota_reserve.go` `TryReserveTokenQuota` 预扣调用 `reserveTokenQuotaDB`——单条 SQL `WHERE remain_quota >= ?` 原子检查+扣减，PostgreSQL 保证并发安全，Redis 不参与权威扣费；(2) Redis Lua 脚本（tokenQuotaReserveScript）只是热路径缓存加速，`GetTokenByKey` miss 即回退 DB；(3) `syncReservedTokenQuotaCache` 是 **DB 预扣成功后异步同步到 Redis**，同步失败则 invalidate 缓存键，宁可放弃缓存不允许缓存与 DB 不一致；(4) 结算 `BillingSession.Settle` → `model.Decrease/IncreaseTokenQuota` → `persistTokenQuotaDelta` 走 DB（或 BatchUpdate 写 DB 队列）；(5) 钱包 `TryReserveUserQuota` 纯 DB 同一模式。**结论**：多实例并发扣费由 DB UPDATE 原子性保证；独立 Redis 只有性能损耗（多查 DB），无正确性风险——v3 扩缩方案此前提成立。
- On 2026-09-05, the deepseek-v4 extreme-sampling official auto-pin was REMOVED; the official pin (`ContextKeyV4OfficialPin`) is now controlled solely by the user's Official Fit `route` dimension. Background: the mainland China site showed a same-session bouncing between aggregator channels 1/2 for `deepseek-v4-flash-vision-exp` because the client's request params (thinking/logprobs/extreme sampling class) auto-pinned every request to the official DeepSeek channel; with the official channel disabled, the distributor's pin guard cleared the aggregator channel-affinity entry on every request (`KeepOnChannelDisabled=false`), so channel affinity could never stick and upstream prompt caches fragmented across aggregators. The user did NOT enable Official Fit, so the params-driven auto-pin violated their expectation that Official Fit controls the behavior. Changes: `markV4OfficialPinFromDistributor` keeps only the `profile.Route` family pin plus the Validate-dimension unknown-model aborts (the K08 extreme-sampling/thinking/logprobs block and the `v4OfficialPinSampling` fields are gone — `Route` already pins the whole family, making the params logic dead); the relay-side `markDeepSeekV4OfficialPin` and its call in `GetAndValidateTextRequest` were removed (relay-time marking could only affect retry selection, which the distributor Route pin already covers); `encoding/json` import dropped from distributor.go; the kimi-k3 Route pin path is unchanged. Tests rewritten: `TestMarkV4OfficialPinFromDistributorNoSamplingAutoPin` (extreme+thinking+logprobs without profile stay unpinned), `TestMarkV4OfficialPinFromDistributorRouteOnlySource` (Validate-only profile never pins), `TestDeepSeekV4ThinkingAndLogprobsDoNotAutoPin` (end-to-end relay validation never pins without Route); `relay/helper/deepseek_v4_pin_test.go` deleted and its `floatPtr` helper moved into `glm53_official_fields_test.go`. `TestMarkV4OfficialPinFromDistributorHonorsRouteProfile` and `TestV4OfficialPinBypassesAggregatorAffinity` unchanged (Route users still pin the family and bypass aggregator affinity). docs/official-fit-mode.md route bullet updated (route = only pin source). Behavioral note: users without the Route dimension now keep channel affinity on aggregators even with thinking/logprobs/extreme sampling — accepted trade-off of the previous "aggregators diverge on K08" protection; docs/official-fit-mode.md records the rationale. Verification: `go build ./...`, full `go test ./...` (all pass), `-race` on middleware pin/affinity tests, `git diff --check` (two pre-existing gofmt const-alignment hunks in valid_request.go were on HEAD and left untouched). Local only: not committed, not pushed, not deployed.

No production target deployment is claimed. Do not retry rc33 against the current overseas database without a reviewed migration compatibility fix or a release based on the current production line; no secrets or response bodies were stored.
- **2026-09-06 海外 rc33 升级完成**：为修复蓝绿 standby 在共享 PostgreSQL 上启动时因 GORM 将历史 `authz_roles.key` 唯一索引误识别为列级 UNIQUE、重复执行 `DROP CONSTRAINT uni_authz_roles_key` 而失败的问题，将 `model/AuthzRole.Key` 的 tag 从 `uniqueIndex` 调整为 `unique`，提交 `4dd467711` 并推送 `origin/tokeness/main`。本地 `go test ./...`、`go vet ./...`、`go build ./...`、独立 `relaykit` 测试和 `git diff --check` 通过；GitHub 发布 workflow `33981326118` 成功，镜像 `v1.0.0-rc.33-tokeness-overseas.1` digest 为 `sha256:f83a038a9c19a7ab369e212ecc39a36215e4bc6d523b968d10c7973f8ec0b388`。首次部署因 JP-N2 根盘满自动恢复 rc42；仅清理未引用 dangling Docker 镜像释放 2.724 GB（磁盘 100%→49%，运行容器保持 healthy）后，同一 digest 的 gated 部署 workflow `33982139570` 成功。最终四节点均运行目标 digest 且 healthy，JP-M 蓝绿状态 committed；`https://tokeness.ai/api/status` 返回 200，`n.tokeness.dev` 与 `n-cf.tokeness.dev` 的 `/v1/models` 按预期返回 401，均暴露版本 `v1.0.0-rc.33-tokeness-overseas.1`。未读取或记录任何 secret 值。
- **2026-09-06 empty-final-content affinity eviction fix (`c112362b7`, pushed `origin/tokeness/main`, NOT tagged/released)**: The frequent Codex report "stream disconnected before completion: upstream returned empty final content" was diagnosed from JP-M PG logs as upstream reseller pools silently dropping accepted requests (35-125s silence through the reasoning phase, then clean EOF with zero events/usage; ~400-700/day platform-wide, 85% on `/v1/responses`, 0.2-4.4% per channel), amplified by (a) `general_setting.ping_interval_enabled=true` (10s) committing the SSE writer before the first upstream byte, so `shouldRetry` never retries streams and `RetryTimes=6`/`AutomaticRetryStatusCodes` are bypassed for anything slower than one ping, and (b) `codex cli trace` channel affinity pinning conversations by `prompt_cache_key` to channel+key with SwitchOnSuccess and no failure eviction, so Codex auto-retries re-entered the same broken channel until TTL expiry (observed: 6 consecutive failures in 9 minutes for one conversation while other users succeeded on the same channel). Fix: `types.ErrOptionWithEmptyOutput()` + `IsEmptyOutput()` marker set by `emptyChatCompletionError`/`emptyResponsesStreamError`, and `service.EvictChannelAffinityOnEmptyOutput` (called from `processChannelError`, also covers the task loop as a no-op) clears the current binding via `ClearCurrentChannelAffinityCache`; the client's automatic retry re-selects a channel and the next success re-binds it (distributor records affinity only on status <400). A message-based sentinel error does NOT work here: `WithOpenAIError` rebuilds `Err` with `errors.New(openAIError.Message)`, breaking `errors.Is`. HybridCache needs no extra cross-node work: with Redis enabled Get/Set/Delete go straight to Redis; the in-memory hot cache is only the Redis-disabled fallback. Verified: `go build ./...` plus full relay/channel/openai, controller, middleware, cachex, service and relaykit/types test suites green; new tests in `service/channel_affinity_evict_test.go` and `relay/channel/openai/empty_output_error_test.go`.
- **2026-09-07 multi-key scheduled testing + image empty-output billing fix (commit `4879e9e9b`, release `v1.0.0-rc.44-tokeness-multikey-test.1`, digest `sha256:3b57c377b89c56e2b5878b2bbe901198cbb9772a1f8d28a35d9a71a1ce32dcb3`, deployed to all overseas nodes healthy)**: (1) New `multi_key_scheduled_test` system task (`controller/multi_key_scheduled.go`) probes every active credential of each multi-key channel via the shared `runMultiKeyCredentialTests` runner (concurrency 4, 45s probe timeout, 500-key cap per channel, `IncludeDisabled` so recovered keys can rejoin), auto-disables failed keys as `auto_disabled` (new allowed status in `UpdateChannelCredentialStatuses`, reason "scheduled test failed") and re-enables recovered `auto_disabled` keys; `manual_disabled` keys are only probed/re-enabled when `monitor_setting.multi_key_test_reenable_manual` is on. Config keys under `monitor_setting.*`: `multi_key_test_enabled`, `multi_key_test_minutes` (default 60), `multi_key_test_model`, `multi_key_test_channels` (comma ID allowlist), `multi_key_test_reenable_manual` — edited from `/system-settings/models/routing-reliability` "Multi-key scheduled testing" block; manual trigger `POST /channel/multi_key/scheduled_test` shares the per-type task dedup. (2) Multi-key dialog: `Test enabled`/`Test all` now send the selected probe model (ComboboxInput fed by channel models + test_model, custom values allowed — backend `model` param already existed); new keyword filter matches http_status/error_code/error_class/error_message/status/disabled_reason (case-insensitive substring, e.g. "Monthly usage limit reached") and non-empty filters switch to the expanded 100-key fetch; the old nested-ternary oxlint errors at `requestedKeyCount` and the Testing button are pre-existing baseline, not from this change. (3) Image billing fix: upstream 200 image responses with `data: []` now fail via `emptyImageResponseError` (`ErrOptionWithEmptyOutput`, committed streams add SkipRetry) in all three OpenAI image paths (`OpenaiImageHandler`, `OpenaiImageStreamHandler` native SSE on clean end with zero completed events, `openaiImageJSONAsStreamHandler`), so `ImageHelper` returns an error, the pre-consume refunds, and the relay retries/fails instead of `PostTextConsumeQuota` billing a full image price for zero output (previously `ImageHelper` forced TotalTokens=1 guaranteeing billability). Side effect: SSE streams with an upstream error event but no completed image now return the empty-output error instead of silently succeeding — `TestOpenaiImageStreamHandlerRecordsUpstreamErrorEvent` and `TestOpenaiImageStreamHandlerForwardsSSEAndUsage` expectations were updated accordingly. Verification: full `go test ./...`, `go vet ./...`, relaykit GOWORK=off test/build, frontend typecheck + 246 vitest tests + production build, i18n sync parity (13 new keys in 7 locales, order-preserving insertion — do NOT re-sort locale files, the JSON keys are not stored in strict localeCompare order and a full re-sort creates a 1200-line diff churn). Deployment: publish run `34053097092` (env approval `tokeness-release` via pending_deployments API, `environment_ids` must be a real JSON array) then deploy run `34053886045` (env approval `tokeness-production`); all nodes + tokeness.ai + n.tokeness.dev/n-cf.tokeness.dev expose `v1.0.0-rc.44-tokeness-multikey-test.1`. Untracked `cn-compat-v7.log` left in place. 国内站未部署（走 CNB tag 流程，需另行发起）。
- **2026-09-07 国内站发布 `v1.0.0-rc.33-tokeness-mainland.11`（commit `4879e9e9b`，与海外 rc.44 同一提交；digest `sha256:0ae357f7f4c29262eaa312126bf68dde17d6e685a1bc4e238f6b0851a87502e2`）**：tag push → CNB tag_push 构建不可变 `ml-<tag>`（tag 推送后约 3 分钟镜像即就绪，registry `/service/token` 两步鉴权可直接查 digest 无需等 CNB UI）。`deploy.sh deploy-release` gated master-first 流程一次通过：SWAS-2 host 容器先升级并 READY（版本校验过）→ ESS 弹 2 等 `/health/ready` → 缩 1 → nginx upstream 自动收敛到新 ECI `10.0.0.235`（收敛窗口出现 3 次 "expected exactly one active server" 为双成员期间预期行为，第 4 次验证通过）→ EIP `8.133.230.82` 自动并入共享带宽包。postcheck：公网版本 `v1.0.0-rc.33-tokeness-mainland.11`、head 渲染正常、`/v1/models` 401。**运维坑（已解决，复用步骤）**：WSL 内 `/mnt/e/`（Windows DrvFs）上私钥 0777 被 ssh 拒绝导致首次 deploy-release 在 master-first 门禁失败——脚本自动回滚 scaling config，生产无损；修复：`cp` 密钥到 WSL 本地 `~/.ssh/swas-ml` + `chmod 600`，之后 `SWAS_SSH_KEY_PATH=/home/vhbs/.ssh/swas-ml` 重跑 deploy-release。CNB registry token 在 `private/access/cnb-token.env`（`CNB_REGISTRY_TOKEN`），WSL 里 `set -a; source ...; set +a` 后调用 deploy.sh。
- **2026-09-07 国内站"CNB 按钮即部署"改造（commit `50a001d07`，已推送 origin）**：应用户要求消除本地机器依赖、保留显式点击。`.cnb/tag_deploy.yml` 的 `cn-production` 部署事件现在在 digest 认证后**在 CNB CI 内直接执行完整 gated 发版**：stage1 认证 `ml-<tag>` digest 并落盘 `/tmp/certified-digest`；stage2 重解析比对（防 stage 间漂移）→ 安装 aliyun CLI（`aliyuncli.alicdn.com` latest tgz，deploy.sh 只用 Describe/Modify 基础 API，3.x 内稳定）→ 从 CNB 加密环境变量解出 SSH 私钥/known_hosts（`tr -d '\r'` 防御 + chmod 600）→ `deploy.sh deploy-release $TAG $CERTIFIED_DIGEST`（master-first 门禁/自动回滚不变）→ stage3 postcheck。配套改动：`deploy-release` 新增可选第三参数（已认证 digest，跳过 registry token 查询，本地两参形式不变）；4 个必需 CNB 仓库加密环境变量缺失时 `:?` fail closed：`ALIBABA_CLOUD_ACCESS_KEY_ID/_SECRET`（RAM 子账号，最小权限 ESS/ECI/VPC）、`CNB_SWAS_SSH_KEY_B64`（swas-ml 私钥 base64）、`CNB_SWAS_KNOWN_HOSTS_B64`（两台 SWAS `ssh-keyscan -H` 输出 base64）。deploy-test.sh 新增 certified-digest 用例（传入 digest ≠ registry 解析值，断言钉的是传入值）。**使用流程变为：推 tag → CNB UI 点 cn-production 部署按钮 → CI 全程执行；本地 deploy.sh 保留为 break-glass。前置条件**：SWAS 两台（8.133.172.195 / 101.133.234.135）22 端口需对 CNB runner 出口公网可达（key-only 认证不变；CNB runner IP 不固定，若安全组做了白名单需放开）；CNB 仓库环境变量需按 README 清单配置，未配置时按钮流程会 fail closed（本地路径不受影响）。验证：WSL 下 deploy-test.sh 基线与改动版均通过、bash -n/YAML parse、线上 postcheck 仍 healthy（mainland.11）。Windows Git Bash 直接跑该测试套件会异常退出（本机环境问题，CI/WSL 正常）。
- **2026-09-07 国内站"CNB 按钮部署"凭据与流程配置完成（commits `50a001d07`/`81b2e96ba`/`4ab4ac41d`，均已推送 origin）**：国内发版链路现为「推 tag → CNB 点 cn-production 部署按钮 → CI 全程执行」，不再依赖本地机器。`.cnb.yml` 的 `tag_deploy.cn-production` 三 stage：certify（解析 ml-<tag> 不可变 digest 并落盘比对）→ release（imports 密钥 → 安装 aliyun CLI → `deploy.sh deploy-release $TAG $CERTIFIED_DIGEST`，master-first 门禁+自动回滚不变）→ postcheck。deploy-release 新增可选第三参数（CI 已认证 digest 直传，跳过 registry token 查询；本地两参数形式不变）。CNB 没有 repo-settings 环境变量（官方 feedback issue #3983 证实），唯一官方密钥方案是**密钥仓库**（secret 类型仓库：禁 clone/本地推送、强制 Web 编辑、水印+引用审计），流水线经 `imports` 注入并以文件头 `allow_slugs/allow_events/allow_branches` 做引用鉴权。密钥仓库 `imvhb/tokeness-secrets` 已由用户登录后经浏览器自动化创建，密钥文件 `cnb-tokeness-secrets.yml`（commit `3da66c1f`）内容四项验证通过：现有企业 AK（用户决定不建最小权限子账号，已接受宽权限残余风险，缓解=allow_* 限定+私有单成员；已用该值验证 ESS API OK）+ SWAS 发版私钥 b64（~/.ssh/swas-ml 同值）+ 两台 SWAS 主机 known_hosts b64。**坑**：(1) 新建文件时文件名输入框被清空，文件落成默认名 cnb-tokeness-secrets.yml，.cnb.yml 已指向它；(2) CNB Web 编辑器是 Monaco，自动化注入 = dom_cua 聚焦 textarea.inputarea + dom_cua.type 全文，提交按钮是 div.t-button（span.t-button__text 父级）；(3) 创建密钥仓库时 radio 被自定义 span 遮挡，需点可见文字标签。SWAS 防火墙核查：两台 22/TCP 对公网全开放（SWAS-2: 80/443/22/ICMP/8300，SWAS-1: 80/443/22/ICMP/6379），CNB runner 可直连。本地留档：`private/access/cnb-deploy-setup-guide.md`（完成态配置记录：架构图/组件位置/残余风险/发版操作/排障坑）与 `private/access/cnb-tokeness-secrets.yml`（与线上一致的本地副本）。发版操作：`git tag v<semver>-tokeness-mainland.<N+1> && git push origin <tag>` → CNB 仓库页点 cn-production 部署按钮 → 盯 release stage 的 SSH 连通性 → 核对 postcheck 版本号。本地 break-glass 路径保留：WSL 里 `deploy.sh deploy-release <tag>`（SWAS_SSH_KEY_PATH=/home/vhbs/.ssh/swas-ml）。

- **2026-09-08 发票种类（普通发票/增值税专用发票）**：新增正交字段 `invoice_kind`（`general`/`special`，空默认为 general），与开票主体 `invoice_type`（individual/company）并存。专票（special）规则：仅限企业（organization），且地址/电话/开户行/账号四字段必填，否则拒绝；前端选专票自动切到企业并禁用个人项。`invoices` 表新增 `invoice_kind` 列（AutoMigrate）；后端 `NormalizeInvoiceKind`/`IsValidInvoiceKind`/`invoiceKindOrGeneral`，错误 `ErrInvoiceSpecialRequiresOrg`/`ErrInvoiceSpecialRequiresFullInfo` + i18n 7 语言。前端：表单「发票种类」单选、专票必填校验、用户/管理员详情与管理员表格展示。i18n 键 `Invoice Kind`/`Ordinary Invoice`/`VAT Special Invoice` 等。
- **合并验证结果**：rc35 合并后的兼容改动已修复上述 `relaykit/dto/channel_settings.go` 值接收者检查与多密钥调度调用问题；`go test ./... -run '^$'` 编译检查、发票 model/controller 聚焦测试均通过。完整 controller 测试在 Windows 并行 SQLite 场景受文件锁清理与 Telegram 测试超时影响，需在稳定环境复核。
- **2026-09-08 发票申请手续费（对外命名「手续费」，非「税金」）实现**：发票申请默认收取申请金额 6% 的手续费，从用户 quota 余额扣除；申请被 `rejected`/`cancelled` 时退还（仅从 pending 进入的这两种终态退款，`issued` 不退）。换算基准 = `round(total_amount × common.QuotaPerUnit × rate)`（fee_quota），展示 `fee_amount = round2(total × rate)`（发票币种）。新增选项 `InvoiceFeeRate`（默认 0.06，0~1 校验，越界 fail-closed 到 0 即免手续费）；`invoices` 表新增 `fee_rate/fee_amount/fee_quota` 列（GORM AutoMigrate 加列）。余额不足返回 `invoice.insufficient_balance` 并阻止申请（不扣成负数）；扣/退走事务内 `debitUserQuotaTx`/`creditUserQuotaTx`（`model/user.go`），事务提交后 `invalidateUserQuotaCacheAfterMutation`。前后端字段命名用 fee（`fee_rate`/`fee_amount`/`fee_quota`），i18n 文案用「手续费/Handling Fee」。
- On 2026-09-08, channel test probes now resolve `{client_header:<name>}` placeholders instead of dropping them. `processHeaderOverride` no longer skips client_header placeholders when `IsChannelTest` is set, and `applyHeaderOverridePlaceholders` falls back to a non-empty probe value (session/conversation/context/connection-like -> UUID, user-agent -> a probe UA, otherwise a probe string) when the synthesized test request carries no real client header. This lets connectivity probes exercise upstreams that gate access on a session or agent header (e.g. OpenCode Go's `X-Opencode-Session`); passthrough rules (`*`, `re:`) are still skipped during tests. Refined in `4f09952fb`: the fallback applies only when the caller supplies a request context (`c != nil && c.Request != nil`). The upstream model-list fetch (`applyFetchModelsHeaderOverrides`) deliberately passes a nil context because it only wants static overrides, so it now leaves the header unset instead of sending a fabricated probe value — do not restore an unconditional fallback or that path starts inventing headers.
- **2026-09-08 CNB push 流水线"静默失败"根因与修复（commit `131ba4619`）**：rc35 批次后 `400f5873b`/`2afd1b350` 两次 push 构建在 `validate mainland deployment tooling` 阶段失败且无可见错误——根因是 CNB 缺省构建镜像 `cnbcool/default-build-env`（Debian trixie，仅 git/jq/rsync/curl/vim/gettext-base）**没有 python3**，而 rc35 同步引入的 `deploy-release` 测试用例走 `resolve_ml_digest`（require_command python3）和 `config_args.py` 序列化器；deploy-test.sh 把每个用例 stderr 重定向进 `state/output.log`，`die` 报错不可见、`set -e` 静默退出（本地 WSL 有 python3 一直通过，掩盖了问题）。此前 9-07 国内发版 mainland.11 走的是本地 WSL break-glass，CNB 按钮生产发版从未实跑，所以 `tag_deploy.cn-production` 同样的坑（序列化器/readback 校验/host 版本检查均需 python3）是潜伏的，首次按钮发版必炸。修复：新增 `deployment/tokeness-cn/ci/Dockerfile.cnb`（default-build-env + python3），`.cnb.yml` 的 tokeness/main push 管线与 tag_deploy.cn-production 均挂 `docker.build` 指向它（CNB 按 Dockerfile 内容哈希缓存镜像，只在文件变化时重建）；deploy-test.sh 开头显式 `command -v python3` 检查 fail loudly。**教训**：CNB CI 上 bash 集成测试报错被 per-case 重定向吞掉时，先查 runner 镜像缺哪个命令（`require_command` die 的输出进 output.log 而非控制台）；本地全绿不代表 CI 绿，环境差异以 CNB 官方镜像清单为准。

- **2026-09-09 国内站 Passkey 二次验证永久失败（根因：`6f2333990` 硬编码 UV=required + Microsoft Password Manager 同步 passkey 断言 UV=false；未改代码，已用管理员重置解绑）**：现象是 `channel.key.read`（渠道密钥查看）与 `passkey.delete` 弹安全验证后一律返回 `SECURITY_VERIFICATION_FAILED / Verification failed. Please try again.`，密码与 Passkey 两种方式都失败，审计表无任何 `category=security` 记录。**根因（字节级证据）**：`6f2333990`（2026-09-06，首次随 `mainland.12` 上线）在 `PasskeyVerifyBegin`/`PasskeyLoginBegin` 把 UV 硬编码为 `WithUserVerification(protocol.VerificationRequired)`，并在三个 finish handler 加 `sessionData.UserVerification != protocol.VerificationRequired` 拒绝；而该账号的凭证 AAGUID `d3452668-01fd-4c12-926c-83a4204853aa` = **Microsoft Password Manager**（Edge 同步 passkey，非设备绑定），其断言返回的 authenticatorData flags=`0x19`（UP=1、**UV=0**、BE=1、BS=1），go-webauthn 的 `AuthenticatorData.Verify` 因 UV 未置位返回 `protocol.ErrVerification`，被 `writeSecurityOperationError` 归入通用 `SECURITY_VERIFICATION_FAILED`。**注意注册是成功的**（`user.passkey_register True`，注册时 UV=true、DB `user_verified=t`），失败只发生在断言阶段，所以现象是"能绑上、用不了"。**时间线佐证**：DB `last_used_at=2026-09-05T11:42:06+08:00`（重构前一天），重构 `6f2333990` 提交于 2026-09-06；`git merge-base --is-ancestor 6f2333990 v1.0.0-rc.33-tokeness-mainland.16` 为真，`.13`~`.17` 均受影响。**管理端开关是死代码**：`passkey.user_verification`（线上 `preferred`，Admin UI 有下拉）只在 `BuildWebAuthn` 里写入 `AuthenticatorSelection`，被上述 `WithUserVerification(required)` 覆盖，且 finish 的硬检查连 `preferred` 会话也一并拒绝。**附带锁号风险（重要）**：`securityVerificationPolicy(auth.login)` 在 `HasPasskey && !HasTwoFA` 时只返回 passkey，`setupLogin` 见到 challenge 就不发会话——即"唯一凭证是 UV-less passkey"的账号**密码登录也会被卡死**；解除绑定后 `methods` 为空 → `StartLoginVerification` 返回 `(nil,nil)` → 密码登录直接发会话（本次已验证 user 1 恢复）。**本次处置**：`DELETE /api/user/1/reset_passkey`（管理 PAT，`canManageTargetRole` 需 root 或高于目标角色；该接口同时 `RevokeAllUserSessions` + 递增 authVersion），执行两次（首次解绑→用户重绑→再次失败→再解绑），终态 `passkey_credentials` 0 行、`/api/user/passkey` 返回 `enabled:false`，审计留下 `user.reset_passkey True`。**修复方向（已于同日实施，见下一条）**：① 让 step-up/login 的 UV 策略读 `passkey.user_verification` 配置（finish 改为按"请求时声明的要求"比对，而非硬要 `required`），代价是敏感操作在 `preferred` 下不再保证二次因子；② 保留 `required` 但对 UV=false 返回区分性错误（如"该认证器不支持用户验证"）而不是通用失败，避免用户反复重试；③ 不改代码的绕过：Windows 设置 > 账户 > 通行密钥 > 高级选项里**关闭 Microsoft Password Manager**，再重新创建 passkey（走设备绑定的 Windows Hello，UV=true）。复现/排查命令：`curl /api/user/passkey`、`/api/audit?category=security`，以及解码断言 `authenticatorData` 的 flags 字节。
- **2026-09-09 上述 Passkey UV 缺陷已修复（采纳方案①+②，未部署）**：改动 6 文件。**单一事实来源**：`service/passkey/service.go` 新增 `ExpectedUserVerification()`（读 `passkey.user_verification`，空/未知值回落 `preferred`）和 `UserVerificationSatisfied(requirement, userVerified)`，`BuildWebAuthn` 改为调用前者，消除了原先"配置写了但被硬编码覆盖"的死代码。**6 处硬编码全部撤除**：`controller/passkey.go` 的 `PasskeyRegisterBegin`（删掉 `selection.UserVerification = required` 覆盖，恢复 `wa.Config.AuthenticatorSelection` 自然生效）、`PasskeyLoginBegin`、`PasskeyVerifyBegin` 三个 begin 改用 `ExpectedUserVerification()`；`PasskeyRegisterFinish`/`PasskeyLoginFinish`/`PasskeyVerifyFinish` 三个 finish 把 `sessionData.UserVerification != required` 的"检查声明值"改为 `UserVerificationSatisfied(sessionData.UserVerification, <响应实际 UV 标志>)`——注意 finish 必须读**断言返回的实际标志**（注册读 `parsedCredential.Response.AttestationObject.AuthData.Flags.HasUserVerified()`，断言读 `parsedCredential.Response.AuthenticatorData.Flags.HasUserVerified()`），不能只看 session 声明值，否则又是"声明 required 就放行"的假校验。`controller/login_verification.go` 的 `LoginPasskeyBegin`/`LoginPasskeyFinish` 同样处理。**可操作错误**：新增 `passkeysvc.ErrUserVerificationUnsupported` + 错误码 `PASSKEY_USER_VERIFICATION_UNSUPPORTED`（`controller/secure_verification.go` 映射 + `web/src/lib/server-error-message.ts` 英文文案；该映射表的文案不经过 i18n 语言包，直接显示英文，故只加英文即可），替代原先含糊的 `SECURITY_VERIFICATION_FAILED`。**安全取舍（重要）**：默认 `preferred` 下 UV=false 的断言现在会被接受，即敏感操作不再保证第二因子（WebAuthn 规范下 `preferred` 本就只保证持有所在设备）；要恢复强保证，把 Admin → 系统设置 → 认证 → Passkey 的 user verification 设为 `required`，此时 UV=false 会返回新错误码并提示用户换认证器。**测试**：`TestSecurityLoginRegisteredPasskeyHonorsConfiguredUserVerification` 重写为 `preferred`/`required` × `verified`=false/true 四组合矩阵（断言 UV-less 在 preferred 下成功、在 required 下失败且 code 为 `PASSKEY_USER_VERIFICATION_UNSUPPORTED`），`beginSecurityLoginPasskey` 等辅助函数改为断言 `passkeysvc.ExpectedUserVerification()` 而非硬编码 `"required"`。**验证**：`go test ./...` 全绿、`go vet ./...` 干净、前端 `vitest src/features/auth` 9 文件/71 例全绿；前端 `tsc --noEmit` 仅剩 2 个既有基线错误（`vendor-management.test.tsx:262`、`access-token-card.test.tsx:88`，均为 `mockImplementation` 参数类型推断，文件未被本次改动触碰）。新错误码的公开契约（脱敏、不泄漏包装细节）由 `TestSecurityEnrollmentPublicErrorsDiscardWrappedDetails` 表驱动覆盖。**OWASP ASVS 5.0.0 对照**：本改动属认证流（WebAuthn），相关要求为协议级校验必须服务端强制、不得由前端替代（前端映射表只做文案展示，判定始终在 Go 侧）；`preferred` 下接受 UV=false 会降低该凭证的保证等级（ASVS 对 MFA/凭证绑定等级的要求），这是显式管理员取舍而非默认削弱——若部署环境需要第二因子保证，必须把 `passkey.user_verification` 设为 `required`。**未部署**：改动只在工作区（`tokeness/main`），线上仍 `mainland.17`；发布后需重新创建 passkey（原凭证已重置）。
- **2026-09-09 retry status codes fully configurable (previously hardcoded in shouldRetry)**: The Routing Reliability page's "Auto-retry status codes" (`AutomaticRetryStatusCodes`) could not disable retries for upstream 400, because `controller/relay.go` `shouldRetry` returned true for any upstream (non-`ErrorTypeNewAPIError`) 400 before ever consulting the configured ranges; `shouldRetryTaskRelay` had the same hardcoded `400 -> true`, and 504/524 were permanently non-retryable via `alwaysSkipRetryStatusCodes`. Fix: two new options replace the hardcoding. `ForceRetryStatusCodes` (default `400`) = always retry another channel for these upstream codes; `NeverRetryStatusCodes` (default `504,524`) = never retry, taking precedence over both the force list and `AutomaticRetryStatusCodes`. Both are parsed/validated/merged by the existing `ParseHTTPStatusCodeRanges` (setting/operation_setting/status_code_ranges.go), registered in model/option.go (load + update branches), validated in controller/option.go, and edited on the Routing Reliability section with live normalization hints; en/zh/zh-TW/fr/ja/ru/vi locales updated. `ShouldRetryByStatusCode` now applies only never-retry + the automatic ranges, and `IsForceRetryStatusCode` is a separate explicit check so local (platform) 400 validation errors stay non-retryable - the force rule is gated on `GetErrorType() != types.ErrorTypeNewAPIError` in `shouldRetry`, while the task path keeps its `LocalError` guard. `IsAlwaysSkipRetryStatusCode` was removed (no callers remain); `IsAlwaysSkipRetryCode` (error-code based, `bad_response_body`) is unchanged and still non-configurable. Defaults reproduce the legacy behavior exactly, so no behavior change without admin action. Verified: go build ./..., go vet on changed packages, gofmt clean, full `go test ./...` green plus relaykit standalone tests, new tests `TestForceRetryStatusCodes{,Configurable}`, `TestNeverRetryStatusCodes{,Configurable}`, `TestUpstreamBadRequestRetryFollowsForceRetryStatusCodes`, `TestNeverRetryStatusCodesOverrideAutomaticRetry`; frontend `bun run build` succeeds and i18n sync reports missing 0. Pre-existing and untouched: 2 frontend typecheck errors (vendor-management/access-token-card tests) and 6 vitest files that use node:test (no suite found), both present before this change. Local only, not committed or deployed.
- **2026-09-10 rc35 merge regression sweep — five fork changes lost, all restored (local only, not committed/deployed)**: A full audit of the `93783d7dc` "sync upstream v1.0.0-rc.35" merge (method below) found five distinct fork-side changes the merge silently dropped. Four were restored this day; the fifth was diagnosed as a deliberate upstream redesign. Attribution note: an earlier write-up credited the hourly series fix to fork commit `5c7cca015` — that is WRONG, `5c7cca015` is an **upstream** commit (author CaIon, not an ancestor of any fork-side commit); the real defect was that the merge produced a mismatched pair (frontend took upstream's `recent_success_series`, backend kept fork/base's `recent_success_rates`).
  (1) **Status strip all-gray despite a shown percentage.** `ModelPerfBadge` (web) and `PerfModelSummary` (web types) expect `recent_success_series` — timestamped hourly points the badge places into 24 client-anchored slots. After the merge the frontend read `recent_success_series` (upstream side) while `pkg/perf_metrics/{types,metrics}.go` still emitted `recent_success_rates` (`[]float64`, last 3 non-empty buckets, no timestamps), so the field name no longer matched and every slot stayed gray; the percentage text still rendered because it reads the separate `success_rate` field. Fix: align the backend to the frontend contract — `SuccessRatePoint` + `RecentSuccessSeries` in types.go and `recentSuccessSeries` merging sub-hour buckets into hour-aligned points (skipping zero-request hours) in metrics.go, keeping the `AvgTtftMs` aggregation from fork's `7f6490468`.
  (1b) **Model-card latency showed total latency instead of TTFT, and the merge left dead `getEffectiveBillingRatio`-style orphans.** Fork's `7f6490468` ("show average TTFT on model card perf badge") made the card read `avg_ttft_ms` under the `TTFT short` label; the rc35 card shows `avg_latency_ms` under `Latency short` while the backend still computes `avg_ttft_ms` and all 7 locale files still carry the now-orphaned `TTFT short` / `Average TTFT` keys ("producer still runs but the consumer was reverted" — the same tell as the dead `formatPriceParts`). Restored: `ModelPerfBadgeData.avg_ttft_ms` plus the `Average TTFT` / `TTFT short` label rendering. TTFT is only sampled for streaming requests, so mostly non-streaming models show `—s` (accepted trade-off recorded in entry 823); the details Performance tab still reports total latency.
  (2) **Raw `<tnt ...>` markup leaking into model description and tags.** `model-card.tsx` rendered `model.description`, `parseTags(model.tags)`, and `model.vendor_name` — all raw authored fields — instead of the already-computed localized fields. The `layout="..."`-looking fragments in the card were `parseTags` splitting raw `<tnt l="zh">免费</tnt>` markup on whitespace and joining with `, `. A prior fix `d52791bf5` ("fix: localize pricing model tags") had switched the card to `localized_tags`, but the `0c76e4dae` model/vendor rework reverted it. Fix follows the existing `localized_tags` boundary (data layer localizes once; consumers render, no re-resolution): `use-pricing-data.ts` additionally computes `localized_description` via `resolveTntContent(model.description || '')`, `pricing/types.ts` declares it, `model-card.tsx` renders `localized_tags` / `localized_description` / `vendor_localized_name`, and `filterBySearch` searches `localized_description` instead of raw `description` (raw searchable text would otherwise expose hidden translations + markup). `model-details.tsx` header already resolves the description via `resolveTntContent` and was left unchanged. Card title/labels unchanged.
  (3) **Original-price strikethrough disappeared from model cards.** The fork card rendered prices via `formatPriceParts`/`formatRequestPriceParts` (which return the undiscounted `original` whenever the displayed group ratio ≠ 1) plus `entry.original` for dynamic entries, showing the original struck through. The upstream card the merge installed used the plain `formatPrice`/`formatRequestPrice` return strings, dropping all three strikethrough sites (`model-card.tsx` line-through count 1→0); `lib/price.ts` and `lib/dynamic-price.ts` kept computing `original` (dead code, comments still say "so the UI can strike through the original"), and `pricing-columns.tsx` / `model-details.tsx` kept their own strikethroughs, so only the card regressed. Restored the three sites in `model-card.tsx` (token-based input/output/cache via `formatPriceParts`, per-request via `formatRequestPriceParts`, dynamic via `entry.original && !entry.formattedRange`), keeping the newer label-above/value-below layout; this also makes the `*Parts` helpers live again. Card tests `strikes through the undiscounted price…` / `…per-request price…` added (assert `.line-through` presence/absence by group ratio, not currency text).
  (4) **Quota/top-up log localization lost + 442 missing locale keys.** Fork's `format.ts` (the merge kept it) had no quota-operation delegation, while upstream rc35 added an audit rewrite (`3f8a50cf8`, author CaIon) whose `renderAuditContent` delegates quota actions to `buildQuotaAuditOperation`, plus 442 new locale keys. Because the merge kept fork's `format.ts` AND fork's locale files, `quota-audit-operation.ts` became an unreachable module, `details-dialog.tsx` lost its top-up localized body and "Quota adjustment details" field section, and every audit/quota string fell back to English. Fix: rebuilt `format.ts` as the union (upstream file + fork's `getEffectiveBillingRatio`, which `common-logs-columns.tsx`/`details-dialog.tsx` and `billing-ratio.test.ts` still consume, + fork's `channel.used_quota_reset` template that the Go side still emits via `controller/channel.go`), ported the two `details-dialog.tsx` pieces, and merged all 442 upstream-only keys into the 7 locale files (fork wording preserved: `模型对话` not upstream's `游乐场`).
  (5) **Setup-guide collapse test superseded, not a regression.** Fork's `c40799463` added `setup-guide-collapse.test.tsx` asserting a `'Setup guide complete'` banner; upstream's later `521cebf58` (2026-09-06, newer than fork's 08-18 rework) deliberately redesigned the completed guide into a bare header toggle and ships its own passing `setup-guide.test.tsx`. The string no longer exists anywhere in code, so the stale fork test was deleted rather than forced to pass.
  **Verification**: `go build ./...` clean, `gofmt` clean (one pre-existing `controller/channel_pin_retry_test.go` gofmt diff left untouched); `go test ./pkg/perf_metrics/...` green incl. new `TestRecentSuccessSeriesAlignsPointsToHoursAndSkipsEmptyHours`; frontend typecheck unchanged (2 pre-existing baseline errors), oxlint clean except one pre-existing React-purity warning in `model-perf-badge.tsx` (confirmed present with the change stashed), oxfmt clean on all changed files; locale merge validated by requiring a byte-identical round-trip before any write (`json.dumps(..., ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)` reproduces these files exactly once the obfuscated `footer.new\u0061pi.projectAttributionSuffix` key is re-escaped). Test results: `bun run test` **12 failed / 701 passed**, down from 31 failed at the start of this sweep. `bun test src/features/usage-logs` went 17 failing → 0 failing. **Not fixed, deliberately**: the 12 remaining failures are upstream-authored test files that were never ported to fork's implementation (`model-cards.test.tsx` and `pricing-controls.test.tsx` do not exist on the fork side at all; they assert USD-formatted prices while the fork card renders ¥, and expect upstream's `Table view` toolbar wording). They are pre-existing rc35 baseline failures, not new, and reconciling them means updating upstream tests — a separate decision.
  **Methods worth reusing after any rcNN sync**: (a) per-file tree-blob comparison `python3` over `git ls-tree -r` of the fork parent / upstream parent / base / merge result yields the exact "result == upstream while fork differed" set (rc35: 10 files, all verified by hand); (b) a per-commit presence scan over `git log upstream..fork` (332 fork-only commits) flags any commit whose added code lines are mostly absent from HEAD; (c) **orphaned producers are the cheapest tell** — a helper, comment, locale key, or i18n string that no longer has a consumer (dead `formatPriceParts`, `TTFT short`, `quota-audit-operation.ts`) almost always means a merge dropped its consumer; (d) the project's `bun run test` runner stops after the vitest phase when vitest fails, so the 68 `node:test` files are effectively invisible in CI — enumerate and run them explicitly (`grep -rl "from 'node:test'" src | tr -d '\r'` then `bun test <file>` one at a time, because bun's path filter also matches sibling `.test.tsx` files under vitest). **The operational rule that mandates checks (a)–(d) after every upstream sync now lives in `AGENTS.md` → "Upstream Sync (rcNN merges)"**; this entry is the case study behind it.
- **2026-09-10 dual-site production release of the rc35 reconciliation (overseas `v1.0.0-rc.45-tokeness-intl.4`, mainland `v1.0.0-rc.33-tokeness-mainland.18`; both shipped from commit `a64e6a494`)**. Overseas: `gh workflow run tokeness-publish.yml -f version=v1.0.0-rc.45-tokeness-intl.4` (approve `tokeness-release`, env id 18799338808) → immutable digest `sha256:383577462ecfcf38909aac3911a56443f99787abfd4db3ee415dc01254085de4` → `gh workflow run tokeness-deploy.yml -f operation=deploy -f image_digest=... -f confirmation=deploy-production` (approve `tokeness-production`, env id 18799354801) → four-node blue-green success, rollout log ends with `deployment complete: all nodes and CDN routes expose version v1.0.0-rc.45-tokeness-intl.4`. The publish step also runs `publish-web-dist.sh`, which switches the CDN: `l1i1/newapi-webdist` `manifest.json` now has `current=v1.0.0-rc.45-tokeness-intl.4`, `previous=v1.0.0-rc.45-tokeness-intl.3`, `source_commit=a64e6a494`; the frontend therefore ships in the same publish, not the deploy. Mainland: annotated tag `v1.0.0-rc.33-tokeness-mainland.18` at `a64e6a494` pushed to the internal origin (mirrors to cnb and GitHub); the CNB `tag_push` build `cnb-c2o-1k250jkvl` went success end-to-end (validate → build immutable image → push → chained `api_trigger` `deploy to production`, ~292s, unattended, no environment approval) and resolves to digest `sha256:4089d3066d0918df44ac66f2866496b550deaea6303d4ae868900499554b3856`. Rollback targets: overseas `intl.3` = `sha256:ca51b913e48c7b4ebdee10c338585bf1dad5fd01277d9ab440298e10bad74404`; mainland `.17` = `sha256:4385b682f733eb86411fdd26d3caa5506b2b93fc8b4a7125d1a7a6823cd1bf42`. Verified on both sites: `/api/status` reports the new version, `/v1/models` returns 401, `/health/live` and `/health/ready` return 200; overseas `/api/perf-metrics/summary` shows all 46 models carrying both `recent_success_series` and `avg_ttft_ms`, confirming the backend contract fix from the entry above is live; `bash deployment/tokeness-cn/deploy.sh postcheck` (WSL) passed. Notes: the mainland `/api/perf-metrics/summary` and `/api/pricing` are behind `requireAuth: true` so they answer `AUTH_UNAUTHORIZED` anonymously — expected, not a fault; `deploy.sh` refuses Windows Git Bash (run postcheck from WSL); the overseas deploy log contains a `rolling back 1 updated node(s)` line that is the deploy test-suite exercising the rollback path, not a production rollback.
- **2026-09-10 configurable error-text retry keywords (`AutomaticRetryKeywords`)**. Operator report: an Ollama channel returning `status_code=400, ollama channel: /v1/responses endpoint not supported` appeared to fail the request outright instead of failing over. Two findings. (1) That specific class is already covered: endpoint-capability failures are typed `channel:unsupported_endpoint`, `types.IsChannelError` makes `shouldRetry` return true before any status-code rule, and `ShouldDisableChannel` exempts it, so it fails over on the deployed rc45 (commit `a64e6a494`) -- the reported log line is the per-attempt `processChannelError` line, not the final response. (2) The general gap was real: any other error whose *text* clearly means "this channel cannot serve the request" but whose status code sits outside the automatic ranges (e.g. 408), or which an adaptor reports as a plain conversion error wrapped with `ErrOptionWithSkipRetry` (codex/newapi/submodel `endpoint not supported`), stayed non-retryable and needed a code change. Added `setting/operation_setting/retry_keywords.go` with `AutomaticRetryKeywords` (newline-separated, lowercased, empty default), `AutomaticRetryKeywordsToString/FromString`, `MatchesAutomaticRetryKeywords` (case-insensitive substring). `shouldRetry` places the keyword check **after** the hard gates (committed response, retry budget, affinity, channel constraints, `specific_channel_id`) and **before** `IsSkipRetryError`, guarded by `!IsAlwaysSkipRetryCode && !IsNeverRetryStatusCode`; it therefore intentionally overrides the generic skip-retry marker and a local error type, which is what makes it useful for conversion-error capability gaps. `shouldRetryTaskRelay` applies the same keyword rule to non-local task errors after its never-retry guard. Full precedence: committed response > channel error > retry budget / affinity / constraints / pin > retry keyword > skip-retry marker > never-retry status code > always-skip error code > force-retry status code > automatic status-code ranges (the last three between the marker and the ranges keep their original relative order). Registered as option `AutomaticRetryKeywords` (`model/option.go` `InitOptionMap` + `updateOptionMap`), default empty so no behavior changes until an operator opts in; surfaced in Routing Reliability as the "Auto-retry error keywords" textarea with 6 new i18n keys (zh / zh-TW translated, fr / ja / ru / vi backfilled from English by `i18n:sync`, which is its documented behavior). Verified: `gofmt` clean on changed files, `go build ./...`, `go vet` on controller/model/setting, `go test ./controller/ ./setting/operation_setting/ ./model/` all green including `TestShouldRetryOllamaResponsesEndpointUnsupported` (pins the reported message), `TestShouldRetryUpstreamErrorMatchingKeyword`, `TestRetryKeywordIsBoundedByHardGates`, `TestRetryKeywordOverridesConversionSkipRetry`, `TestShouldRetryTaskRelayFollowsConfiguredKeywords`, and the setting-layer keyword tests; `bun run build` clean; `bun run i18n:sync` reports `missingCount: 0` / `extrasCount: 0` for all 7 locales. Local only -- not committed, published, or deployed.
- **2026-09-10 rc36 同步（上游 `v1.0.0-rc.36` = `ea7cb0ba4`，仅隔离分支，未推送未部署）**: 在隔离 worktree `E:/Ai/Worktrees/newapi-rc36-20260910`（分支 `codex/sync-upstream-rc36`，起点 `a6c960fd8`）用 `git merge --no-commit --no-ff` 合并；46 个冲突文件按 union 逐个人工合并，未使用 `checkout --ours/--theirs` 整文件取一侧。7 个 locale 按 key 并集重建（本地 6578 + rc36 6143 → 6740 键，7 语言 key 集一致、零重复、本地译文全部保留）。`AGENTS.md` 的 rcNN 规则补上“测试入口必须两阶段都执行”。
  **确认并修复 2 个真实回归**：rc36 的 `0e0ba152b` 重写 `web/src/features/pricing/lib/billing-expr.ts` 的 `parseTiersFromExpr`，加入“只接受 p/c/len 条件的完整线性 tier 链”严格校验，于是时段峰谷价表达式 `weekday("Asia/Shanghai") <= 5 ? tier("pro_peak", ...) : tier("pro_offpeak", ...)` 被判为不可摘要而返回空；下游 `dynamic-price.ts` 的 `getDisplayedTier`（fork 侧来自 `e5d0abc4c`）拿不到任何 tier，卡片/详情再也显示不出当前峰谷档。同一表达式在 rc35（fork HEAD）能被宽松正则匹配到两个 tier，故这是**合并引入的回归**而非既有缺陷。修法：严格实现改名 `parseLinearTierChain`，外层 `parseTiersFromExpr` 新增 `parseTimeConditionalTiers` 兜底（仅当前者为空、且条件全为 `hour/minute/weekday/month/day("TZ")` 与数字比较、两支各解析出恰好 1 个 tier 时生效），复用同文件已有的 `unwrapOuterParens` 与 `findTaskTopLevelCharacter`。验证：`bun test src/features/pricing/lib/__tests__/dynamic-price.test.ts` 4/4 通过（合并后原为 2 fail）。
  **第 2 个真实回归（同一类：rc36 改共享工具、fork 整文件覆盖）**：rc36 的 `model-price-cell.tsx` 是**新增组件**，其设计是「价格只出数字、货币由 caption 出一次」，因此 rc36 给 `web/src/features/pricing/lib/price.ts` 的 `formatPrice`/`formatRequestPrice` 各加了末位参数 `showCurrencySymbol`（默认 true）。`price.ts` 在本次合并中取的是 fork 版（fork 的末位参数是 `displayCurrency`），于是 rc36 组件里 `formatPrice(..., false)` 的调用被改成 `formatPrice(..., options.displayCurrency)`——结果同一个单元格里价格带 `¥`、caption 又写 `USD`，货币出现两次且两者不一致；`model-listing.test.tsx`（rc36 新增测试）8 个断言失败。修法：把 `showCurrencySymbol` 作为**追加在 fork 的 `displayCurrency` 之后**的末位参数补回 `price.ts`（`formatPricingCurrencyFromUSD` 的 options 增加 `showSymbol?: boolean`），`model-price-cell.tsx` 三处调用改为 `displayCurrency, false`；同时把该组件内部用于数值换算的 `displayCurrency` 与它自己渲染的 caption 对齐（`currencyLabel === 'CNY' ? 'CNY' : 'USD'`，即与 `getConfig()` 的 `quotaDisplayType` 覆盖保持一致），避免「caption 写 USD、数值按 ¥ 汇率换算」。验证：`model-listing.test.tsx` 由 8 failed → 3 failed，`model-cards`(6 failed)/`pricing-controls`(3 failed) 与 HEAD 基线逐项一致，未引入新失败。**注意这与 fork 的 `web/src/lib/currency.ts` 无关**：该文件 rc35→rc36 上游零改动、fork 自己改了 73 行，其中 `getConfig()` 用 `quotaDisplayType: displayCurrency` 无条件把管理端配置的 USD/CNY/CUSTOM/TOKENS 覆盖成用户偏好；rc36 与 fork 的新测试都断言 CUSTOM(🐱)/TOKENS 可用，因此这批失败是既有分歧而非合并丢失。若要修，候选是一行条件覆盖（仅当 `baseConfig.quotaDisplayType === 'USD'` 时才用用户偏好），但会改变全站货币显示策略，应单独立项并做视觉/回归验证。
  **测试入口修复**：`web/scripts/run-tests.mjs` 原先在 vitest 失败后直接退出，68 个 `node:test` 文件永不执行；改为两阶段都跑、退出码累加，并把 bun 的测试参数改成 `./` 前缀的精确相对路径（避免 bun 的路径过滤同时命中同名 vitest 文件）。修复后立刻暴露 3 个此前不可见的 `node:test` 失败，其中 2 个正是上面的峰谷价回归。
  **基线对照方法（可复用）**：`git worktree add --detach <dir> HEAD` + `New-Item -ItemType Junction` 把主 worktree 的 `web/node_modules` 链过去，即可在 HEAD 上跑同一批测试取得基线，无需重装依赖。实测重叠的 6 个上游测试文件在 HEAD 上为 10 failed / 66 passed。
  **遗留（未修，均属上游测试与 fork 实现的既有分歧）**：全量 `bun run test` 后 vitest 22 failed / 1009 passed（修前 26/1005，合并初稿 31/1000），`node:test` 1 failed / 247 passed（唯一的 `vendor management dialog > preserves a disabled vendor status when editing metadata` 在 HEAD 基线上同样失败）。失败集中在 `model-cards`(6)、`metadata-editing`(超时 5，随并行度浮动)、`model-listing`(3)、`pricing-controls`(3)、`vendor-management`、`detail-preview`、`redemptions-export`、`setup-guide`、`audit viewer`（单跑 31/31 通过，属并发污染）。根因同类：fork 的货币显示（`¥` vs 上游无符号）、`formatGroupDiscount` 对 ratio=1 返回 `undefined`（上游测试期望 `Group Ratio 1x`）、上游新增工具栏/分页/导出文案。`node:test` 剩余 1 个 `vendor-management dialog > preserves a disabled vendor status when editing metadata` 在 HEAD 上同样失败，属既有缺陷。
  **验证**：`go build -p 2 ./...`、`go vet -p 2 ./...`、`go test -p 2 ./...`（51 包 ok / 0 FAIL）全绿；`relaykit` 独立 `GOWORK=off go build ./... && go test ./...` 全绿；前端 `bun run typecheck`、`bun run build` 通过。Windows 上并行 Go 链接会 OOM（`VirtualAlloc ... errno=1455`），必须加 `-p 2`，否则出现的 “build failed” 全是资源假象。三套 rcNN 审计（父/结果 blob 比对、362 个 fork-only 提交存活扫描、孤儿生产者扫描）已完成：结果==上游仅 1 个（`api-key-group-cell.tsx`，本地差异只有注释与格式）、高置信度丢失 0。
